## Gold Standard Format Reference (GV-GB-001)

All policies should match this section structure:

| Order | Level | Title | Tab | Required |
| ----: | ----: | :---- | :-- | :------: |
| 1 | 1 | `{Policy Title}` | (cover) | ✓ |
| 2 | 2 | `1\. Policy Header` | Overview | ✓ |
| 3 | 2 | `2\. Purpose` | Overview | ✓ |
| 4 | 2 | `3\. Scope` | Overview | ✓ |
| 5 | 2 | `4\. Policy Statement` | Overview | ✓ |
| 6 | 2 | `5\. Definitions` | Overview | ✓ |
| 7 | 2 | `6\. Procedures` | Procedures | ✓ |
| 19 | 2 | `7\. Documentation Requirements` | Documentation | ○ |
| 20 | 2 | `8\. Compliance & Audit Considerations` | Compliance | ○ |
| 24 | 2 | `9\. References` | References | ○ |
| 29 | 2 | `10\. Training & Acknowledgment Requirements` | Admin | ○ |
| 30 | 2 | `11\. Version Control` | Admin | ○ |
| 31 | 2 | `APPENDICES` | Appendices | ○ |

**Table format:** `| :---- | :---- |` (left-align markers in header separator row)

**Section body separator:** each section body ends with `\n\n---`
